		public MainWindow()
		{
			InitializeComponent();

		[DllImport("attpsr.dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
		public static extern int ATTPSR_getPackTypeByCode(int Code, out CodeNameDesc_Type pPackType);

		[DllImport("attpsr.dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
		public static extern int ATTPSR_getVendorByCode(int Code, ref CodeNameDesc_Type pPacktype, out CodeNameDesc_Type pVendor);

		[DllImport("attpsr.dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
		public static extern int ATTPSR_getMaterialByCode(int Code, ref CodeNameDesc_Type pPacktype, ref CodeNameDesc_Type pVendor, out CodeName_Type pMaterial);

		[DllImport("attpsr.dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
		public static extern int ATTPSR_getPackSizeByCode(int Code, ref CodeNameDesc_Type pPacktype, ref CodeNameDesc_Type pVendor, ref CodeName_Type pMaterial, out CodeName_Type pPackSize);

		[DllImport("KERNEL32.DLL")]
		private static extern IntPtr LoadLibrary(string lpFileName);

		private void Load_ATTPSR()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aprsystem\\41.0\\mm");
			if (registryKey == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = registryKey.GetValue("mmcommonxeq").ToString();
			registryKey.Close();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (!text.EndsWith("\\"))
				{
					text += "\\";
				}
				text += "attpsr.dll";
				LoadLibrary(text);
			}
		}

		private void bOpenFileDlg_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog.Filter = "Batch Modeler Files |*.bspf";
			openFileDialog.FilterIndex = 1;
			openFileDialog.Title = "Find .bspf file";
			openFileDialog.Multiselect = false;
			openFileDialog.CheckFileExists = true;
			openFileDialog.CheckPathExists = true;
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK)
			{
				txtBoxBkpName.Text = "";
				msBspfFullFilePathName = openFileDialog.FileName;
				int num = msBspfFullFilePathName.LastIndexOf('\\');
				msBspfFullFilePath = msBspfFullFilePathName.Substring(0, num + 1);
				msBspfFileName = msBspfFullFilePathName.Substring(num + 1);
				num = msBspfFileName.LastIndexOf('.');
				msBkpFileName = msBspfFileName.Substring(0, num);
				msBkpFullFilePath = msBspfFullFilePath;
				msBkpFullFilePathName = msBspfFullFilePath + msBkpFileName + msBkpExt;
				string value = lOutputBkp.Content.ToString();
				lInputBspf.Content = msBspfFullFilePathName;
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
				{
					lOutputBkp.Content = msBkpFullFilePath;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtBoxBkpName.Text))
				{
					txtBoxBkpName.Text = msBkpFileName + ".bkp";
				}
			}
		}

		private void TextBox_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				string text = txtBoxBkpName.Text;
				text = text.Trim();
				string text2 = (string)lOutputBkp.Content;
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					msBkpFileName = text;
					msBkpFullFilePathName = msBkpFullFilePath + msBkpFileName + msBkpExt;
					lOutputBkp.Content = msBkpFullFilePathName;
					txtBoxBkpName.Text = msBkpFileName;
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void bChangeBkpDir_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			FolderBrowserDialog folderBrowserDialog = new FolderBrowserDialog();
			folderBrowserDialog.SelectedPath = msBkpFullFilePath;
			if (folderBrowserDialog.ShowDialog() == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK)
			{
				msBkpFullFilePath = folderBrowserDialog.SelectedPath + "\\";
				msBkpFullFilePathName = msBkpFullFilePath;
				lOutputBkp.Content = msBkpFullFilePathName;
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void bOpenConvFile_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string text = lOutputBkp.Content.ToString();
			text = text.Trim();
			string text2 = txtBoxBkpName.Text;
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			sFilePathName = "";
			bool flag = false;
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2))
			{
				int num = text2.LastIndexOf('.');
				if (num > 0 && text2.Substring(num + 1).ToLower() == "bkp")
				{
					sFilePathName = text + text2;
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			sFilePathName = sFilePathName.Trim();
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFilePathName) && (sFilePathName.Contains('&') || sFilePathName.Contains('|') || sFilePathName.Contains(';') || sFilePathName.Contains('$') || sFilePathName.Contains('>') || sFilePathName.Contains('<') || sFilePathName.Contains('!')))
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("Aspen Plus File name / path " + text2 + " is not correct or does not exist.", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
				bOpenConvFile.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else if (!flag)
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("Aspen Plus File name " + text2 + " is not correct or does not exist.", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
				bOpenConvFile.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else if (!File.Exists(sFilePathName))
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("Aspen Plus File " + sFilePathName + " does not exist.", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
				bOpenConvFile.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				StartAP();
			}
		}

		private void StartAP()
		{
			stinfo = new ProcessStartInfo("AspenPlus.exe");
			stinfo.FileName = sFilePathName;
			stinfo.UseShellExecute = true;
			Process.Start(stinfo);
		}

		private void bConvertBspfToBkp_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			lstMsgBox.Items.Clear();
			bOpenConvFile.IsEnabled = false;
			bool flag = false;
			if (File.Exists(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			string text = lOutputBkp.Content.ToString();
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Aspen Plus File Directory not found.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			string text2 = txtBoxBkpName.Text;
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Aspen Plus File  not found.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			int num = text2.LastIndexOf('.');
			if (num == -1)
			{
				text2 += ".bkp";
				txtBoxBkpName.Text = text2;
			}
			else if (!(text2.Substring(num + 1).ToLower() == "bkp"))
			{
				text2 += ".bkp";
				txtBoxBkpName.Text = text2;
			}
			msBkpFullFilePathName = text + text2;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (File.Exists(msBkpFullFilePathName))
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("File " + msBspfFullFilePathName + " does not exist.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			if (flag2 && System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("File " + msBkpFullFilePathName + " already exists.\nDo you want to replace it?", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation) == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.No)
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion canceled.");
				return;
			}
			_ = Encoding.ASCII;
			string text3;
			try
			{
				using StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName);
				text3 = streamReader.ReadLine();
				streamReader.Close();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("The file " + msBspfFullFilePathName + "could not be read.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			bool flag3 = false;
			int result = 0;
			int num2 = text3.IndexOf("VERSION", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			string text4;
			if (num2 >= 0)
			{
				List<int> list = new List<int>();
				int startIndex = 0;
				while ((startIndex = text3.IndexOf('"', startIndex)) != -1)
				{
					list.Add(startIndex);
					startIndex++;
				}
				if (list.Count >= 2 && list[0] > num2 + 7)
				{
					text4 = text3.Substring(list[0] + 1, list[1] - list[0] - 1);
					text4 = text4.Trim();
					int num3 = text4.IndexOf('.');
					if (num3 >= 0)
					{
						text4 = text4.Substring(0, num3);
					}
					if (int.TryParse(text4, out result))
					{
						flag3 = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag3 || result < 30)
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show("File " + msBspfFullFilePathName + " is from a release older than V8.4).\nPlease load this file into Aspen Batch Modeler V8.4 or later and save before converting.", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("The first line in .bpsf file : " + text3);
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Unrecognized version. Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			text4 = "\"" + result + ".0\"";
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			string text5 = "ddd MMM d HH:mm:ss yyyy";
			string text6 = now.ToString(text5);
			string value = "MM " + sConverterVer + " FLAVOR \"NO\" VERSION " + sConverterVer + " DATETIME \"" + text6 + "\"";
			string text7 = "";
			using (StreamReader streamReader2 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				bool flag4 = false;
				string text8;
				while ((text8 = streamReader2.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					if (flag4)
					{
						text8 = text8.Trim();
						if (text8.Contains("as") && text8.Contains("BatchSep"))
						{
							int num4 = -1;
							if ((num4 = text8.IndexOf(" ")) > 0)
							{
								text7 = text8.Substring(0, num4);
								text7 = text7.Trim();
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					text8 = text8.Trim();
					if (text8.Contains("FLOWSHEET") && text8 == "FLOWSHEET")
					{
						flag4 = true;
					}
				}
				streamReader2.Close();
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Block name is not defined.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			sDefCompLine = "";
			sCompDefList.Clear();
			using (StreamReader streamReader3 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				bool flag5 = false;
				bool flag6 = false;
				int num5 = 0;
				string text9;
				while ((text9 = streamReader3.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					text9 = text9.Trim();
					if (flag5)
					{
						if (flag6)
						{
							num5++;
							if (text9.Contains("Components :") && num5 == 1)
							{
								sDefCompLine = text9;
								break;
							}
							if (num5 > 1)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
						text9 = text9.Trim();
						if (text9.Contains("Within Default"))
						{
							flag6 = true;
						}
					}
					if (text9 == "Default as ComponentList;")
					{
						flag5 = true;
					}
				}
				streamReader3.Close();
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sDefCompLine))
			{
				string[] array = sDefCompLine.Replace("Components :", "").Trim().Trim(';')
					.Trim()
					.TrimStart('[')
					.TrimEnd(']')
					.Trim()
					.Split(',');
				sCompDefList.Clear();
				int num6 = array.Length;
				for (int i = 0; i < num6; i++)
				{
					array[i] = array[i].Trim();
					array[i] = array[i].Trim('"');
					array[i] = array[i].Trim();
					sCompDefList.Add(array[i].ToUpper());
				}
			}
			string text10 = "";
			using (StreamReader streamReader4 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				bool flag7 = false;
				string text11;
				while ((text11 = streamReader4.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					if (flag7)
					{
						text11 = text11.Trim();
						if (text11.Contains("ID:") && text11.IndexOf("ID") == 0)
						{
							int num7 = -1;
							if ((num7 = text11.IndexOf(" ")) > 0)
							{
								text10 = text11.Substring(num7);
								text10 = text10.Trim();
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					text11 = text11.Trim();
					if (text11 == "BLOCK")
					{
						flag7 = true;
					}
				}
				streamReader4.Close();
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text10))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Block ID is not define.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			msCurrentUOMSet = "";
			bool flag8 = false;
			using (StreamReader streamReader5 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				string text12;
				while ((text12 = streamReader5.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					text12 = text12.Trim();
					if (!text12.Contains("CurrentUOMSet") || !text12.Contains(":") || !text12.EndsWith(";"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					text12 = text12.Trim();
					string[] array2 = text12.Split(':');
					if (array2.Length < 2)
					{
						continue;
					}
					array2[1] = array2[1].Trim();
					if (!array2[1].EndsWith(";"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int length = array2[1].Length;
					if (length < 2)
					{
						continue;
					}
					msCurrentUOMSet = array2[1].Remove(length - 1, 1);
					msCurrentUOMSet = msCurrentUOMSet.Trim('"');
					int num8 = msUOMSystems.Length;
					for (int j = 0; j < num8; j++)
					{
						if (string.Compare(msUOMSystems[j], msCurrentUOMSet, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
						{
							flag8 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				streamReader5.Close();
			}
			if (!flag8)
			{
				string text13 = "Current UOM set";
				text13 = text13 + " (" + msCurrentUOMSet + ") is not found or defined in this tool.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add(text13);
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Please load simulation in Aspen Batch Modeler, change the UOM set, save and then convert it to Aspen Plus.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			int num9 = UOMTable.Length;
			mstrCurrUOMList.Clear();
			msCurrentUOMSet = "Metric";
			for (int k = 0; k < num9; k++)
			{
				UOMtruct item = UOMTable[k];
				if (string.Compare(item.acmUOMset, msCurrentUOMSet, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					mstrCurrUOMList.Add(item);
				}
			}
			FileStream fileStream;
			try
			{
				fileStream = ((!flag2) ? new FileStream(msBkpFullFilePathName, FileMode.CreateNew) : new FileStream(msBkpFullFilePathName, FileMode.Truncate));
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("The file " + msBkpFullFilePathName + "was not created.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion failed.");
				return;
			}
			fileStream.Close();
			List<string> list2 = null;
			using (StreamReader streamReader6 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				bool flag9 = false;
				string text14;
				while ((text14 = streamReader6.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					if (text14.Contains("GRAPHICS_BACKUP"))
					{
						list2 = new List<string>();
						flag9 = true;
					}
					if (!flag9)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (text14.Contains("#"))
					{
						int num10 = sConfigDataArray.Length;
						for (int l = 0; l < num10; l++)
						{
							list2.Add(sConfigDataArray[l]);
						}
						list2.Add(text14);
						flag9 = false;
						break;
					}
					if (text14.Contains("GRAPHICS_BACKUP") || text14.Contains("PFS") || text14.Contains("$CONFIG") || text14.Contains("paramdata") || text14.Contains("purebank"))
					{
						list2.Add(text14);
					}
				}
				streamReader6.Close();
			}
			if (list2 == null)
			{
				_ = "File " + msBspfFullFilePathName + " does not have  GRAPHICS_BACKUP section.\nConversion failed.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("File " + msBspfFullFilePathName + " does not have  GRAPHICS_BACKUP section, can be bad.");
			}
			List<string> list3 = null;
			using (StreamReader streamReader7 = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				bool flag10 = false;
				int num11 = -1;
				string text15;
				while ((text15 = streamReader7.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					string text16 = text15;
					text16 = text16.Trim();
					if (text16.Contains("? SETUP") && !text15.Contains("? SETUP MAIN ?") && !flag10)
					{
						int num12 = text16.IndexOf("? SETUP", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
						if (num12 >= 0)
						{
							text16 = text16.Substring(num12);

			List<string> sGlobalReactionList = new List<string>();
			CreateBkpReactionSection(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sReactionListUpp, ref sGlobalBlockList, ref sGlobalReactionList);
			using (StreamWriter streamWriter = File.AppendText(msBkpFullFilePathName))
			{
				streamWriter.WriteLine(value);
				int num73 = sHedderArray.Length;
				for (int num74 = 0; num74 < num73; num74++)
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine(sHedderArray[num74]);
				}
				streamWriter.WriteLine("");
				int count8 = list6.Count;
				for (int num75 = 0; num75 < count8; num75++)
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine(list6[num75]);
				}
				streamWriter.WriteLine("");
				int count9 = sGlobalBlockList.Count;
				for (int num76 = 0; num76 < count9; num76++)
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine(sGlobalBlockList[num76]);
				}
				streamWriter.WriteLine("");
				int count10 = sGlobalReactionList.Count;
				for (int num77 = 0; num77 < count10; num77++)
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine(sGlobalReactionList[num77]);
				}
				if (list2 != null)
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine("");
					int count11 = list2.Count;
					for (int num78 = 0; num78 < count11; num78++)
					{
						streamWriter.WriteLine(list2[num78]);
					}
				}
				int count12 = msSkipVariables.Count;
				if (count12 > 0)
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Skip variables:");
					for (int num79 = 0; num79 < count12; num79++)
					{
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("  " + msSkipVariables[num79]);
					}
				}
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Conversion finished.");
			}
			System.Windows.Forms.Cursor.Current = System.Windows.Forms.Cursors.Default;
			bOpenConvFile.IsEnabled = true;
		}

		private void CheckDummyString()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			list.Add("R1");
			list.Add("BOT");
			int count = sChargeStreamsINM0_0.Count;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				text = sChargeStreamsINM0_0[i];
				text2 = sDicStreamLongToShort[text];
				text2 = text2.ToUpper();
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				list.Add(text2);
			}
			int num = 1;
			while (list.Contains(sDummyStream))
			{
				sDummyStream += num;
				num++;
			}
		}

		private void FixSetupPot(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			if (!bfPot)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<string> sVariableListUpp2 = new List<string>();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains("POT.") && sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf("POT.", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					sVariableListUpp2.Add(sVariableListUpp[i]);
				}
			}
			string text = "Pot.ModelDetail :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "SHORTCUT";
			}
			string item = "\\ HEATTRANSFER H-TRANS-MODE = SHORTCUT \\ ";
			string item2 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER H-TRANS-MODE = DETAILED \\ ";
			if (sFound == "DETAILED")
			{
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item2);
			}
			if (sFound == "SHORTCUT")
			{
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text = "Pot.ThermalSpec :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "TEMPERATURE";
			}
			string text2 = sFound;
			text = "Pot.QR :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.0";
			}
			string sIn = sFound;
			if (text2 == "DUTY")
			{
				string rowCol = GetRowCol("HeatFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sIn);
				string item3 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HT-SPEC = DUTY HT-DUTY = " + sFound + rowCol + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item3);
			}
			text = "Pot.TR :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "25.0";
			}
			string sIn2 = sFound;
			if (text2 == "TEMPERATURE")
			{
				string rowCol2 = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sIn2);
				string item4 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HT-SPEC = TEMPERATURE HT-TEMP = " + sFound + rowCol2 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item4);
			}
			text = "Pot.CompBasis :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (sFound == "MOLE-FRAC" || sFound == "MOLE-HOLDUP")
			{
				string item5 = "\\ \"COL-CONFIG\" CONFIG = POT CHARGE-BASIS = MOLE \\ ";
				if (bfPot)
				{
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(item5);
				}
			}
			if (sFound == "MASS-HOLDUP" || sFound == "MASS-FRAC")
			{
				string item6 = "\\ \"COL-CONFIG\" CONFIG = POT CHARGE-BASIS = MASS \\ ";
				if (bfPot)
				{
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(item6);
				}
			}
			text = "Pot.VReactor :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.6";
			}
			string rowCol3 = GetRowCol("Volume");
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			string item7 = "\\ \"COL-CONFIG\" CONFIG = POT VREACTOR = " + sFound + rowCol3 + "\\ ";
			if (bfPot)
			{
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item7);
			}
			text = "InitialCond :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "INITIAL CHARGE";
			}
			if (sFound == "TOTAL REFLUX" || sFound == "INITIAL CHARGE")
			{

			}
			string item8 = "\\ INICONDITION POTINITCOND = " + sFound + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(item8);
			text = "Pot.Vent_Vapor :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";

			sFound = sFound.Trim();

			{
				sFound = "FALSE";
			}
			if (sFound == "FALSE")
			{

			}
			if (sFound == "TRUE")
			{

			{

			}

			if (bfPot)

		private void FixVariablesWarning(string sBlockName)
		{
			List<string> list = null;
			using (StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(msBspfFullFilePathName))
			{
				string value = "Task ";
				string text = "End";
				bool flag = false;
				string value2 = sBlockName + ".";
				bool flag2 = false;
				string text2;
				while ((text2 = streamReader.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					text2 = text2.Trim();
					if (text2.Contains(value) && text2.IndexOf(value, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					if (flag && text2.Contains(text) && text2 == text && text2.IndexOf(text, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					if ((!flag || text2.IndexOf(value2) != 0 || !text2.Contains(':') || !text2.Contains(';')) && (!flag || !text2.Contains("ramp") || !text2.Contains(value2) || !text2.Contains(';') || text2.IndexOf("ramp", StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (!flag2)
					{
						list = new List<string>();
						flag2 = true;
					}
					if (text2.Contains("ramp") && text2.IndexOf("ramp", StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						text2 = text2.Replace("ramp", "");
						text2 = text2.Trim();
						text2 = text2.TrimEnd(';');
						text2 = text2.Trim();
						text2 = text2.TrimStart('(');
						text2 = text2.TrimEnd(')');
						text2 = text2.Trim();
						string[] array = text2.Split(',');
						if (array.Length >= 2)
						{
							text2 = array[0].Trim() + " : " + array[1].Trim() + ";";
						}
					}
					if (!text2.Contains("EndTime : Time;"))
					{
						list.Add(text2);
					}
				}
				streamReader.Close();
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = list.Count();
			if (num > 0)
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Warning - The following variables have been modified in an operating step and may be");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("                  incorrect in Aspen Plus :");
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("     " + list[i]);
				}
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Please review these values in Aspen Plus. You can also do following in Aspen Batch Modeler");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("to ensure consistency : Load simulation in Aspen Batch Modeler, reinitialize and save it.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Convert the saved .bspf file to a .bkp file and use in Aspen Plus.");
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("");
			}
		}

		private void GetSDRInfo(ref string sInOut, ref Dictionary<string, string> sDicSRDLongToShort, ref Dictionary<string, string> sDicSRDLongNToStreamN)
		{
			if (!bfAddM1_0StreamOut)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			string text2 = sBkpChargeStreamsLine;
			string text3 = "SDR";
			string text4 = "";
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in sDicSRDLongToShort)
			{
				text = item.Key;
				_ = item.Value;
				if (text == sDummyStream)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < 10000; i++)
				{
					text4 = text3 + i;
					if (!text2.Contains(text4))
					{
						sDicSRDLongNToStreamN.Add(text, text4);
						text2 = text2.Replace(") \\ ", text4 + " M1-0 ) \\ ");
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			sBkpChargeStreamsLine = text2;
		}

		private void CreateBkpReactionSection(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sReactionListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList, ref List<string> sGlobalReactionList)
		{
			int count = sReactionListUpp.Count;
			string text = "ReactionMode :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "POT ONLY";
			}
			string text2 = "";
			if (count == 0 && sFound != "POT ONLY")
			{
				text2 = "\\ REACTION REAC-LOC = # \\ ";
				sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
				text2 = text2.Replace("#", sDicReactMode[sFound]);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			}
			else
			{
				if (count <= 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				text2 = "\\ REACTION REAC-LOC = # \\ ";
				sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
				text2 = text2.Replace("#", sDicReactMode[sFound]);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
				text = "ReactionModelsSet :";
				text = text.ToUpper();
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
				sFound = sFound.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					return;
				}
				sFound = sFound.TrimStart('[');
				sFound = sFound.TrimEnd(']');
				sFound = sFound.Trim();
				sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					return;
				}
				List<string> sNameList = new List<string>();
				string[] array = sFound.Split(',');
				int num = array.Length;
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					array[i] = array[i].Trim();
					array[i] = array[i].Trim('"');
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[i]))
					{
						sNameList.Add(array[i]);
					}
				}
				if (sNameList.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				Dictionary<string, string> sDicLongShort = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				text = "";
				sFound = "R-";
				GetDicLongShort(ref sDicLongShort, ref sFound, ref sNameList);
				if (sDicLongShort.Count() == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				text = "ReactionModel :";
				text = text.ToUpper();
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
				sFound = sFound.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					return;
				}
				text2 = "\\ REACTION REAC-MODEL = # \\ ";
				text2 = text2.Replace("#", sDicLongShort[sFound]);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
				string text3 = "";
				List<string> sVariableListUpp2 = new List<string>();
				List<string> sReactModeStoiclLines = new List<string>();
				new List<string>();
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				Dictionary<string, double> sDicCompNameCoef = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				int count2 = sReactionListUpp.Count;
				text = "";
				List<string> sNameList2 = new List<string>();
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item2 in sDicLongShort)
				{
					string key = item2.Key;
					text3 = item2.Value;
					text2 = sReactH;
					text2 = text2.Replace("#", text3);
					sGlobalReactionList.Add(text2);
					text = key + ".";
					sVariableListUpp2.Clear();
					for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
					{
						if (sReactionListUpp[j].Contains(text))
						{
							sFound = sReactionListUpp[j];
							sFound = sFound.Replace(text, "");
							sFound = sFound.Trim();
							sVariableListUpp2.Add(sFound);
						}
					}
					sReactModeStoiclLines.Clear();
					int count3 = sVariableListUpp2.Count;
					text = "CStoich(";
					text = text.ToUpper();
					for (int k = 0; k < count3; k++)
					{
						if (sVariableListUpp2[k].Contains(text))
						{
							sReactModeStoiclLines.Add(sVariableListUpp2[k]);
						}
					}
					text = "RxnSet :";
					text = text.ToUpper();
					sFound = "";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
					sFound = sFound.Trim();
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						break;
					}
					sFound = sFound.TrimStart('[');
					sFound = sFound.TrimEnd(']');
					sFound = sFound.Trim();
					sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						break;
					}
					sNameList2.Clear();
					string[] array2 = sFound.Split(',');
					num = array2.Length;
					for (int l = 0; l < num; l++)
					{
						array2[l] = array2[l].Trim();
						array2[l] = array2[l].Trim('"');
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array2[l]))
						{
							sNameList2.Add(array2[l]);
						}
					}
					if (sNameList2.Count == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					Dictionary<string, string> sDicLongShort2 = new Dictionary<string, string>();
					text = "";
					sFound = "RXN-";
					GetDicLongShort(ref sDicLongShort2, ref sFound, ref sNameList2);
					int num2 = 1;
					string text4 = "";
					string sFoundI = "";
					string text5 = "";
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item3 in sDicLongShort2)
					{
						string key2 = item3.Key;
						string value = item3.Value;
						text4 = "RxnParam(\"" + key2 + "\").Status :";
						FindReactLineValue(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text4, ref sFoundI);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundI))
						{
							sFoundI = "ON";
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundI))
						{
							text2 = "\\ REAC-DATA R-D-REACNO = # REACNAME = ! STATUS = % \\ ";
							text2 = text2.Replace("#", num2.ToString());
							text2 = text2.Replace("!", value);
							text2 = text2.Replace("%", sFoundI);
							sGlobalReactionList.Add(text2);
						}
						text4 = "RxnParam(\"" + key2 + "\").RxnClass :";
						FindReactLineValue(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text4, ref sFoundI);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundI))
						{
							sFoundI = "POWERLAW";
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundI))
						{
							text2 = "\\ REAC-DATA R-D-REACNO = # REACNAME = ! REAC-CLASS = % \\ ";
							text2 = text2.Replace("#", num2.ToString());
							text2 = text2.Replace("!", value);
							text2 = ((!sDicReatClass.ContainsKey(sFoundI)) ? text2.Replace("%", "POWERLAW") : text2.Replace("%", sDicReatClass[sFoundI]));
							sGlobalReactionList.Add(text2);
							text2 = "\\ REAC-ACT ACT-REACNO = # REAC-CLASS1 = % \\ ";
							text2 = text2.Replace("#", num2.ToString());
							text2 = ((!sDicReatClass.ContainsKey(sFoundI)) ? text2.Replace("%", "POWERLAW") : text2.Replace("%", sDicReatClass[sFoundI]));
							sGlobalReactionList.Add(text2);
							if (sFoundI == "CUSTOM")
							{
								string text6 = "RxnParam(\"" + key2 + "\").Custom(1).";
								text6 = text6.ToUpper();
								List<string> sVariableListUpp3 = new List<string>();
								int count4 = sVariableListUpp2.Count;
								for (int m = 0; m < count4; m++)
								{
									if (sVariableListUpp2[m].Contains(text6) && sVariableListUpp2[m].IndexOf(text6, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
									{
										sVariableListUpp3.Add(sVariableListUpp2[m]);
									}
								}
								string text7 = text6 + "VarNameList :";
								text7 = text7.ToUpper();
								FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp3, ref text7, ref sFound);
								sFound = sFound.Trim();
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
								{
									sFound = sFound.TrimStart('[');
									sFound = sFound.TrimEnd(']');
									sFound = sFound.Trim();
									string[] array3 = sFound.Split(',');
									int num3 = array3.Length;
									for (int n = 0; n < num3; n++)
									{
										array3[n] = array3[n].Trim();
									}
									string text8 = "";
									for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num3; num4++)
									{
										string text9 = array3[num4];
										text7 = text6 + "CustomVars(" + text9 + ").";
										text8 = text7 + "VarType :";
										text8 = text8.ToUpper();
										FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp3, ref text8, ref sFound);
										sFound = sFound.Trim();
										if (sFound == "REACTION PARAMETER")
										{
											text8 = text7 + "VarValue :";
											text8 = text8.ToUpper();
											FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp3, ref text8, ref sFound);
											sFound = sFound.Trim();
											if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
											{
												sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
											}
											text9 = text9.Trim('"');
											text2 = "\\ CUSTOM-PARM PARMID = # PARM-VALUE = ! <0> <0> \\ ";
											string newValue = "";
											text9 = text9.Replace("_", newValue);
											text9 = text9.Replace("-", newValue);
											text2 = text2.Replace("#", text9);
											text2 = text2.Replace("!", sFound);
											sGlobalReactionList.Add(text2);
										}
									}

		private void FindReactDicCompNameCoef(ref Dictionary<string, double> sDicCompNameCoef, ref List<string> sReactModeStoiclLines, string sRLong1)
		{
			string text = "CStoich(";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string text2 = ").Coef(\"" + sRLong1 + "\")";
			text2 = text2.ToUpper();
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int count = sReactModeStoiclLines.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sReactModeStoiclLines[i].Contains(text) && sReactModeStoiclLines[i].Contains(text2))
				{
					list.Add(sReactModeStoiclLines[i]);
				}
			}
			int count2 = list.Count;
			double result = 0.0;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				string[] array = list[j].Split(':');
				if (array.Length <= 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				array[0] = array[0].Replace(text, "");
				array[0] = array[0].Replace(text2, "");
				array[0] = array[0].Trim();
				string[] array2 = array[0].Split(',');
				if (array2.Length > 1)
				{
					array2[1] = array2[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					if (double.TryParse(array[1], out result))
					{
						sDicCompNameCoef.Add(array2[1], result);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void FindReactLineValue(ref List<string> sReactionListUpp, ref string sFindI, ref string sFoundI)
		{
			sFindI = sFindI.ToUpper();
			sFoundI = "";
			int count = sReactionListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sReactionListUpp[i].Contains(sFindI))
				{
					sFoundI = sReactionListUpp[i];
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundI))
			{
				string[] array = sFoundI.Split(':');
				if (array.Length > 1)
				{
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
					sFoundI = array[1];
				}
			}
		}

		private void GetDicLongShort(ref Dictionary<string, string> sDicLongShort, ref string sFound, ref List<string> sNameList)
		{
			string text = "";
			int count = sNameList.Count;
			int num = 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				text = sNameList[i];
				text = text.Replace('_', '-');
				if (text.Length > 8)
				{
					text = sFound + num;
					num++;
					while (sDicLongShort.ContainsValue(text))
					{
						text = sFound + num;
						num++;
					}
					sDicLongShort.Add(sNameList[i], text);
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Long name " + sNameList[i] + " has been replaced by name " + text + ".");
				}
				else if (sDicLongShort.ContainsValue(text))
				{
					text = sFound + num;
					num++;
					while (sDicLongShort.ContainsValue(text))
					{
						text = sFound + num;
						num++;
					}
					sDicLongShort.Add(sNameList[i], text);
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Long name " + sNameList[i] + " has been replaced by name " + text + ".");
				}
				else
				{
					sDicLongShort.Add(sNameList[i], text);
				}
			}
		}

		private void FixHeatTransferConfig(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			text = "Pot.HeatTransfer.HTModeCoils :";
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			text2 = text2.TrimEnd('.');
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text3 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HTMODECOILS = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text = "Pot.HeatTransfer.HTModeExtHX :";
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			text2 = text2.TrimEnd('.');
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text3 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HTMODEEXTHX = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text = "Pot.HeatTransfer.HTModeJacket :";
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			text2 = text2.TrimEnd('.');
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text3 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HTMODEJACKET = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text = "Pot.HeatTransfer.JacketCooling :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			string text4 = text2;
			text = "Pot.HeatTransfer.JacketHeating :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			text2 = text2.Trim();
			string text5 = text2;
			if (text5 == "TRUE")
			{
				text5 = "1";
			}
			else if (text5 == "FALSE")
			{
				text5 = "0";
			}
			if (text4 == "TRUE")
			{
				text4 = "1";
			}
			else if (text4 == "FALSE")
			{
				text4 = "0";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				text3 = (bfBatchDistilColumn ? "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-COOLING = 0 JA-HEATING = 1 \\ " : (bfPotOverCond ? "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-COOLING = 0 JA-HEATING = 1 \\ " : "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-COOLING = 1 JA-HEATING = 0 \\ "));
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				text3 = "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-COOLING = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text4);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				text3 = "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-HEATING = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text5);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				text3 = "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-HEATING = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text5);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
				text3 = "\\ \"HEATTRANSFER\" JA-COOLING = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text4);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text = "HeatCap :";
			text2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref text2);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text2 = "FALSE";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text3 = "\\ HEATTRANSFER HEATCAP = # \\ ";
				text3 = text3.Replace("#", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
		}

		private string GetNStageStr(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp)
		{
			string sFind = "Configuration :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			bfPotOverCond = false;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound) && sFound == "POT + OVERHEAD CONDENSER")
			{
				bfPotOverCond = true;
			}
			string text = "";
			string sFind2 = "NStage :";
			string sFound2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind2, ref sFound2);
			sFound2 = sFound2.Trim();
			text = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2)) ? sFound2 : "10");
			if (bfPotOverCond)
			{
				text = "2";
			}
			return text;
		}

		private int GetNStageN(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (!int.TryParse(GetNStageStr(ref sVariableListUpp), out result))
			{
				result = 0;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void GetNStageESN(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sDefEndStage, ref string sDefStartStage, ref string sNStage)
		{
			sNStage = GetNStageStr(ref sVariableListUpp);
			int result = 0;
			if (!int.TryParse(sNStage, out result))
			{
				result = 0;
			}
			if (result < 2)
			{
				result = 2;
			}
			int num = 2;
			int num2 = result - 1;
			if (num2 < 2)
			{
				num2 = 2;
			}
			sDefEndStage = num2.ToString();
			sDefStartStage = num.ToString();
			sNStage = result.ToString();
		}

		private void SetNStage(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string nStageStr = GetNStageStr(ref sVariableListUpp);
			string text = "\\ \"COL-CONFIG\" NSTAGE = # \\ ";
			text = text.Replace("#", nStageStr);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
		}

		private void GetNStage(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sDefEndStage, ref string sDefStartStage, ref string sNStage)
		{
			string sFind = "NStage :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			sDefEndStage = "";
			sDefStartStage = "2";
			sNStage = "";
			int result = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sNStage = sFound.Trim();
				if (!int.TryParse(sNStage, out result))
				{
					result = 0;
				}
			}
			if (result > 0)
			{
				sDefEndStage = (result - 1).ToString();
			}
			else
			{
				sDefEndStage = "9";
				result = 10;
				sNStage = "10";
			}
			if (result < 2)
			{
				sDefStartStage = result.ToString();
			}
			int result2 = 0;
			int.TryParse(sDefStartStage, out result2);
			int result3 = 0;
			int.TryParse(sDefEndStage, out result3);
			if (result3 < result2)
			{
				sDefEndStage = sDefStartStage;
			}
		}

		private void FixHeatLoss(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string sDefEndStage = "";
			string sDefStartStage = "";
			string sNStage = "";
			GetNStageESN(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sDefEndStage, ref sDefStartStage, ref sNStage);
			int result = 0;
			int.TryParse(sNStage, out result);
			if (result <= 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<string> sVariableListUpp2 = new List<string>();
			text = "HeatLossSections(";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					sVariableListUpp2.Add(sVariableListUpp[i]);
				}
			}
			text = "HeatLossSections(";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			text2 = "";
			Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, int>();
			int count2 = sVariableListUpp2.Count;
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp2[j].Contains(text))
				{
					text2 = sVariableListUpp2[j];
					text2 = text2.Trim();
					int num2 = text2.IndexOf('(');
					int num3 = text2.IndexOf(')');
					string text3 = text2.Substring(num2 + 1, num3 - num2 - 1);
					text3 = text3.Trim();
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(text3))
					{
						num++;
						dictionary.Add(text3, num);
					}
				}
			}
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			string text6 = "";
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, int> item in dictionary)
			{
				text2 = "";
				text5 = sDefStartStage;
				text = "HeatLossSections(" + item.Key + ").StartStage :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref text2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = text5;
				}
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-STARTSTG = ! \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", item.Value.ToString());
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
				text2 = "";
				text5 = sDefEndStage;
				text = "HeatLossSections(" + item.Key + ").EndStage :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref text2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = text5;
				}
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-ENDSTG = ! \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", item.Value.ToString());
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", text2);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
				text2 = "";
				text5 = "0.5";
				text4 = GetRowCol("HeatTransferCoeff");
				text4 = text4.Trim();
				text = "HeatLossSections(" + item.Key + ").UEnv :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref text2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = text5;
				}
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-LOSSU = ! ? \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", item.Value.ToString());
				text2 = TrancateTo18(text2);
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", text2);
				text6 = text6.Replace("?", text4);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
				text2 = "";
				text5 = "2";
				text4 = GetRowCol("Area");
				text4 = text4.Trim();
				text = "HeatLossSections(" + item.Key + ").AEnv :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref text2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = text5;
				}
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-LOSSA = ! ? \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", item.Value.ToString());
				text2 = TrancateTo18(text2);
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", text2);
				text6 = text6.Replace("?", text4);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
			}
			if (dictionary.Count == 0)
			{
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-STARTSTG = ! \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", "1");
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", sDefStartStage);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
				text6 = "\\ HT-HEATLOSS HL-SEC = # HL-ENDSTG = ! \\ ";
				text6 = text6.Replace("#", "1");
				text6 = text6.Replace("!", sDefEndStage);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text6);
			}
		}

		private void FixHeatCapacity(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string sDefEndStage = "";
			string sDefStartStage = "";
			string sNStage = "";
			GetNStageESN(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sDefEndStage, ref sDefStartStage, ref sNStage);
			int result = 0;
			int.TryParse(sNStage, out result);
			if (result <= 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<string> sVariableListUpp2 = new List<string>();
			text = "HeatCapSections(";
			text = text.ToUpper();

			text2 = "";

		private void FixProcessSideHeatTrCoef(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string sFind = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FilmHTCOptPot :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "FIXED";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (sFound == "FIXED")
			{
				text = "\\ HEATTRANSFER P-FILMHTCOPT = FIXED \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(sFound == "SIEDER-TATE CORRELATION"))
				{
					return;
				}
				text = "\\ HEATTRANSFER P-FILMHTCOPT = CORRELATION \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				sFind = "Pot.HeatTransfer.AgitatorType :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "FLAT BLADE DISC TURBINE";
				}
				switch (sFound)
				{
				case "ANCHOR":
				case "BAFFLED 3 BLADE TURBINE":
				case "FLAT BLADE DISC TURBINE":
				{
					string sFind2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.AgitatorCondition :";
					string sFound2 = "";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind2, ref sFound2);
					if (sFound == "ANCHOR")
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound2))
						{
							sFound2 = "30 < RE < 300";
						}
						sFound2 = ((!(sFound2 == "30 < RE < 300")) ? "ANCHOR2" : "ANCHOR1");
					}
					if (sFound == "BAFFLED 3 BLADE TURBINE")
					{
						sFound2 = ((!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound2) && !sFound2.Contains("GLASSED STEEL")) ? "BAFFLED1" : "BAFFLED2");
					}
					if (sFound == "FLAT BLADE DISC TURBINE")
					{
						sFound2 = ((!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound2) && !(sFound2 == "RE < 400")) ? "DISC1" : "DISC2");
					}
					if (sDicAgitatorType.ContainsKey(sFound))
					{
						text = "\\ HEATTRANSFER AGIT-TYPE = ! AGIT-CONDIT = ? \\ ";
						sFind = sDicAgitatorType[sFound];
						text = text.Replace("!", sFind);
						text = text.Replace("?", sFound2);
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
					}
					break;
				}
				default:
					if (sDicAgitatorType.ContainsKey(sFound))
					{
						text = "\\ HEATTRANSFER AGIT-TYPE = ! AGIT-CONDIT = ! \\ ";
						sFind = sDicAgitatorType[sFound];
						text = text.Replace("!", sFind);
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
					}
					break;
				}
				sFind = "POT.HEATTRANSFER.AGITATORSPEED :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
				{
					string rowCol = GetRowCol("AngularVelocityRPM");
					text = "\\ HEATTRANSFER AGIT-SPEED = #?\\ ";
					text = text.Replace("#", sFound);
					text = text.Replace("?", rowCol);
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				}
			}
		}

		private void FixConvergence(ref List<string> sOptionsList, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			if (sOptionsList == null || sOptionsList.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			string sFind = "Integrator:";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				if (sFound == "EULER" || sFound == "RK4")
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Integrator " + sFound + " is not supported in A+. Default integrator \"Implicit Euler\" will be used.");
				}
				else
				{
					text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" INTEG-METHOD = ! \\ ";
					text = ((!(sFound == "GEAR")) ? text.Replace("!", "IMPLICIT") : text.Replace("!", sFound));
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				}
			}
			sFind = "Integration.InitStepSize:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" INITVARSIZE = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.MinStepSize:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" MINVARSIZE = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.MaxStepSize:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" MAXVARSIZE = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.RelErrorTol:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" RELTOLINTEG = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.AbsErrorTol:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" ABSTOLINTEG = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "TimeSettings.CommunicationInterval:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.01";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				string rowCol = GetRowCol("Time");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" COM-INTERVAL = " + sFound + rowCol + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.ItplToComTime:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = ((!(sFound == "FALSE")) ? "1" : "0");
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" INTERPOLATE = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Nonlinear.MaxIter:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" NLA-ITER = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Nonlinear.HiResidual:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" NLA-HIRES = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Nonlinear.HiVarSteps:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" NLA-HIVARSTP = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.ReInitAfterIE:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				sFound = ((!(sFound == "FALSE")) ? "1" : "0");
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" REINIT-AFTER = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "Integration.ShowHIErrors:";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sOptionsList, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ \"SOLVERS-OPT\" HIERRORS = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
		}

		private void FixFinalConditions(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string sFind = "DrumFinalCond :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "LEAVE IN PLACE";
			}
			text = "\\ FINALCOND DRUMFINALCON = " + sFound switch
			{
				"LEAVE IN PLACE & RECYCLE" => "RECYCLE", 
				"DUMP TO DISTILLATE RECEIVER" => "RECEIVER", 
				"LEAVE IN PLACE" => "PLACE", 
				_ => "PLACE", 
			} + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			sFind = "DrumFinalCondRec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ FINALCOND DRUMFINALREC = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "InternalsFinalCond :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "LEAVE IN PLACE";
			}
			text = "\\ FINALCOND INTERNFINCON = " + sFound switch
			{
				"LEAVE IN PLACE & RECYCLE" => "RECYCLE", 
				"DUMP TO DISTILLATE RECEIVER" => "RECEIVER", 
				"LEAVE IN PLACE" => "PLACE", 
				_ => "PLACE", 
			} + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			sFind = "InternalsFinalCondRec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ FINALCOND INTERNFINREC = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
			sFind = "PotFinalCond :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "LEAVE IN PLACE";
			}
			text = "\\ FINALCOND POTFINALCOND = " + sFound switch
			{
				"LEAVE IN PLACE & RECYCLE" => "RECYCLE", 
				"DUMP TO DISTILLATE RECEIVER" => "RECEIVER", 
				"LEAVE IN PLACE" => "PLACE", 
				_ => "PLACE", 
			} + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			sFind = "PotFinalCondRec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ FINALCOND POTFINALREC = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
			}
		}

		private void FixCondenser(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string text = "Condenser";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					list.Add(sVariableListUpp[i]);
				}
			}
			text = "Condenser.CondenserType :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			text = "Condenser.CondType :";
			string sFound2 = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound2);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2) ? "TOTAL" : sFound2);
			}
			if (!sDicCondenserType.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "TOTAL";
			}
			string text2 = "\\ \"COL-CONFIG\" CONDENSER = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicCondenserType[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser.PartialSpec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "TEMPERATURE";
			}
			if (!sDicPartialSpec.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "PARTIAL";
			}
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER PARTIALSPEC = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicPartialSpec[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser.SubCSpec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "NONE";
			}
			if (!sDicSubCSpec.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "NONE";
			}
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER SUBCSPEC = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicSubCSpec[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser.vfR :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				return;
			}
			string rowCol = GetRowCol("MoleFraction");
			double result = 0.0;
			if (double.TryParse(sFound, out result))
			{
				if (result > 1.0 || result < 0.0)
				{
					result = 0.5;
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser: Value(" + sFound + ") of variable \"vfR\" out of range [0-1].");
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser: Value of variable \"vfR\" set to default (0.5).");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = 0.5;
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser: Value of variable \"vfR\" set to default (0.5).");
			}
			sFound = TrancateTo18(result.ToString());
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER VFR = " + sFound + rowCol + "\\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
		}

		private void FixCondenser2(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			List<string> sVariableListUpp2 = new List<string>();
			string text = "Condenser2(1).";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					sVariableListUpp2.Add(sVariableListUpp[i]);
				}
			}
			text = "Condenser2(1).CondenserType :";
			string sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "PARTIAL";
			}
			if (!sDicCondenserType.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "PARTIAL";
			}
			string text2 = "\\ CONDENSER2 CONDENSER2 = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicCondenserType[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser2(1).PartialSpec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "TEMPERATURE";
			}
			if (!sDicPartialSpec.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "PARTIAL";
			}
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER2 PARTIALSPEC2 = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicPartialSpec[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser2(1).SubCSpec :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp2, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "NONE";
			}
			if (!sDicSubCSpec.ContainsKey(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "NONE";
			}
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER2 SUBCSPEC2 = $ \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("$", sDicSubCSpec[sFound]);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Condenser2(1).vfR :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				return;
			}
			string rowCol = GetRowCol("MoleFraction");
			double result = 0.0;
			if (double.TryParse(sFound, out result))
			{
				if (result > 1.0)
				{
					result = 0.5;
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser2: Value(" + sFound + ") of variable \"vfR2\" out of range [0-1].");
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser: Value of variable \"vfR2\" set to default (0.5).");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = 0.5;
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Condenser2: Value of variable \"vfR2\" set to default (0.5).");
			}
			sFound = TrancateTo18(result.ToString());
			text2 = "\\ CONDENSER2 VFR2 = " + sFound + rowCol + "\\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
		}

		private void FixExternalExchangeCooling(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			text2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.ExtHXCoolOpt :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
			text = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE");
			if (!sDicCoolingOptions.ContainsKey(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			text3 = "\\ EXTERNALCOOL XC-OPT = ! \\ ";
			text4 = sDicCoolingOptions[text];
			text3 = text3.Replace("!", text4);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			text2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedCoolExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "20.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text5 = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text3 = "\\ EXTERNALCOOL XC-TMED = " + sFound + text5 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedInCoolExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "20.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text5 = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text3 = "\\ EXTERNALCOOL XC-TMEDIN = " + sFound + text5 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.DutyCoolExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text5 = GetRowCol("HeatFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text3 = "\\ EXTERNALCOOL XC-DUTY = " + sFound + text5 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			_ = text4 == "TEMP";
			if (text4 == "LMTD")
			{
				text2 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptCoolExtHx :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "SPECIFY PROPERTIES";
				}
				if (sFound == "SPECIFY PROPERTIES")
				{
					text3 = "\\ EXTERNALCOOL XC-PROPOPT = PROPERTY \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
				}
				else
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptCoolExtHx : : \"Use thermal fluid library\" is not implemented.");
				}
			}
			_ = text4 == "DUTY";
		}

		private void FixCoilsCooling(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			string text6 = "";
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.CoilsCoolOpt :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			text2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE");
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.UOptCoolCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			text6 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "OVERALL");
			if (sDicCoolingOptions.ContainsKey(text2))
			{
				text4 = "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-OPT = ! CC-UOPT = ? \\ ";
				text5 = sDicCoolingOptions[text2];
				text4 = text4.Replace("!", text5);
				text4 = text4.Replace("?", text6);
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FilmHTCOptCoolCoils :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "FIXED";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text4 = ((!(sFound == "FIXED")) ? "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-FILMHTCOP = CORRELATION \\ " : "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-FILMHTCOP = FIXED \\ ");
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptCoolCoils :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "SPECIFY PROPERTIES";
				}
				if (sFound == "SPECIFY PROPERTIES")
				{
					text4 = "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-PROPOPT = PROPERTY \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				else
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptCoolCoils : : \"Use thermal fluid library\" is not implemented.");
				}
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedCoolCoils :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "20.";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
					sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
					text4 = "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-TMED = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedInCoolCoils :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "20.";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
					sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
					text4 = "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-TMEDIN = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.DutyCoolCoils :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "0.";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = GetRowCol("HeatFlow");
					sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
					text4 = "\\ COILSCOOLING CC-DUTY = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				_ = text5 == "DUTY";
				_ = text5 == "TEMP";
				_ = text5 == "LMTD";
			}
		}

		private void FixExternalExchangeHeating(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.ExtHXHeatOpt :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			text2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE");
			if (!sDicHeatingOptions.ContainsKey(text2))
			{
				return;
			}
			text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-OPT = ! \\ ";
			text5 = sDicHeatingOptions[text2];
			text4 = text4.Replace("!", text5);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "120.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-TMED = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedInHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "120.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-TMEDIN = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FmBoilupHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MassFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-FMBOILUP = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FBoilupHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MoleFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-FBOILUP = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.DutyHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("HeatFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-DUTY = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FmSteamHeatExtHX :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MassFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-FMSTEAM = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			_ = text5 == "DUTY";
			_ = text5 == "TEMP";
			if (text5 == "STEAM")
			{
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.ExtHXSteamLimit :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "LATENT HEAT";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text4 = ((!(sFound == "MAXIMUM DUTY")) ? "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-STEAMLIMT = HEAT \\ " : "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-STEAMLIMT = DUTY \\ ");
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
			}
			_ = text5 == "MASS-BR";
			_ = text5 == "MOLE-BR";
			if (text5 == "LMTD")
			{
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptHeatExtHx :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "SPECIFY PROPERTIES";
				}
				if (sFound == "SPECIFY PROPERTIES")
				{
					text4 = "\\ EXTERNALHEAT XH-PROPOPT = PROPERTY \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
				else
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptHeatExtHx : \"Use thermal fluid library\" is not implemented.");
				}
			}
			_ = text5 == "FRAC";
		}

		private void FixCoilsHeating(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text5 = "";
			string text6 = "";
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.CoilsHeatOpt :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			text2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "SPECIFIED MEDIUM TEMPERATURE");
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.UOptHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			text3 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound)) ? sFound : "OVERALL");
			if (!sDicHeatingOptions.ContainsKey(text2))
			{
				return;
			}
			text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-OPT = ! CH-UOPT = ? \\ ";
			text6 = sDicHeatingOptions[text2];
			text5 = text5.Replace("!", text6);
			text5 = text5.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FilmHTCOptHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "FIXED";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text5 = ((!(sFound == "FIXED")) ? "\\ COILHEAT CH-FILMHTCOP = CORRELATION \\ " : "\\ COILHEAT CH-FILMHTCOP = FIXED \\ ");
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "SPECIFY PROPERTIES";
			}
			if (sFound == "SPECIFY PROPERTIES")
			{
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-PROPOPT = PROPERTY \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			else
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptHeatCoils : \"Use thermal fluid library\" is not implemented.");
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "120.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-TMED = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedInHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "120.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-TMEDIN = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FmBoilupHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MassFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-FMBOILUP = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FBoilupHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MoleFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-FBOILUP = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.DutyHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("HeatFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-DUTY = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FmSteamHeatCoils :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetRowCol("MassFlow");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				text5 = "\\ COILHEAT CH-FMSTEAM = " + sFound + text + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
			}
			_ = text6 == "DUTY";
			_ = text6 == "TEMP";
			if (text6 == "STEAM")
			{
				text4 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.CoilsSteamLimit :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text4, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "LATENT HEAT";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text5 = ((!(sFound == "MAXIMUM DUTY")) ? "\\ COILHEAT CH-STEAMLIMT = HEAT \\ " : "\\ COILHEAT CH-STEAMLIMT = DUTY \\ ");
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text5);
				}
			}
			_ = text6 == "MASS-BR";
			_ = text6 == "MOLE-BR";
			_ = text6 == "LMTD";
		}

		private void FixJacketHeating(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)

			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.TMedHeatJacket :";

				sFound2 = TrancateTo18(sFound2);
				text4 = "\\ JACKETHEAT JH-FMBOILUP = " + sFound2 + text6 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.FBoilupHeatJacket :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound2);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2))
			{

				text6 = GetRowCol("MoleFlow");
				sFound2 = TrancateTo18(sFound2);
				text4 = "\\ JACKETHEAT JH-FBOILUP = " + sFound2 + text6 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			text3 = "HeatTransfer.FmSteamHeatJacket :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound2);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2))

				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
			}
			if (text5 == "DUTY")
			{
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.DutyHeatJacket :";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text3, ref sFound2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2))

					text4 = text4.Replace("#", sFound2);

					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);

			}
			if (text5 == "TEMP")
			{
				text3 = "Pot.HeatTransfer.PropOptHeatJacket :";

				}

				{

					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);

				}

				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound2))
				{
