					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text4);
				}
			}
		}

		private void FindLine(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sFind, ref string sFound)
		{
			sFind = sFind.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(sFind) && sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf(sFind, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					sFound = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
		}

		private bool FindVendorLine(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sVendorLine)
		{
			bool result = false;
			string text = "InternalsSections(";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string text2 = ").Internals :";
			text2 = text2.ToUpper();
			string text3 = "Vendor Tray";
			string text4 = "Vendor Packing";
			string text5 = text3.ToUpper();
			string text6 = text4.ToUpper();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (!sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text) || sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf(text, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 || !sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array = sVariableListUpp[i].Split(':');
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
					if (array[1] == text5)
					{
						sVendorLine = text3;
						result = true;
						break;
					}
					if (array[1] == text6)
					{
						sVendorLine = text4;
						result = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void FindLineVal(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sFind, ref string sFound)
		{
			sFind = sFind.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			string text = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(sFind) && sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf(sFind, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					sFound = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetValueR(ref sFound);
				text = text.Trim('"');
				sFound = text.Trim();
			}
		}

		private void FixInitConditions(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string item = "";
			string text3 = "";
			text = "InitialCond :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "TOTAL REFLUX";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				item = ((!sDicInitialCond.ContainsKey(sFound)) ? "\\ INICONDITION INITIALCOND = TOTAL \\ " : ((!sDicInitialCond.TryGetValue(sFound, out text)) ? "\\ INICONDITION INITIALCOND = TOTAL \\ " : ("\\ INICONDITION INITIALCOND = " + text + " \\ ")));
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text2 = "";
			text = "Inert :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					sFound = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text2 = GetValueR(ref sFound);
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				text2 = text2.Trim('"');
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					item = "\\ INICONDITION INERT = " + text2 + " \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int count2 = sCompDefList.Count;
				bool flag = false;
				string text4 = "";
				if (count2 > 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
					{
						if (sCompDefList[j] == "N2" || sCompDefList[j] == "NITROGEN")
						{
							text4 = sCompDefList[j];
							item = "\\ INICONDITION INERT = " + sCompDefList[j] + " \\ ";
							sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
							flag = true;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Initial Conditions: Value of Inert component/pad gas set to default (" + text4 + ").");
				}
			}
			text = "TInitial1 :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text3 = GetRowCol("Temperature");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				item = "\\ INICONDITION TINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text = "PInitial1 :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text3 = GetRowCol("Pressure");
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				item = "\\ INICONDITION PINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text = "DrumVolFracInitial :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text3 = " <0> <0> ";
				sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
				item = "\\ INICONDITION DRUMVOLFRAC = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text = "InitialChargeType :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				if (sFound == "TOTAL FRESH CHARGE")
				{
					item = "\\ INICONDITION CHARGETYPE = FRESH \\ ";
				}
				if (sFound == "TOTAL INITIAL CHARGE")
				{
					item = "\\ INICONDITION CHARGETYPE = INITIAL \\ ";
				}
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			else
			{
				item = "\\ INICONDITION CHARGETYPE = INITIAL \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			}
			text = "Pot.CompBasis :";
			sFound = "";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "MOLE-FRAC";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				string text5 = "";
				if (sFound == "MASS-HOLDUP" || sFound == "MASS-FRAC")
				{
					text5 = "MASS";
				}
				if (sFound == "MOLE-FRAC" || sFound == "MOLE-HOLDUP")
				{
					text5 = "MOLE";
				}
				item = "\\ INICONDITION P-HLDUPBASIS = " + text5 + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
				if (sFound == "MASS-FRAC")
				{
					text = "Pot.MmInitial :";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						text3 = GetRowCol("Mass");
						sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
						item = "\\ INICONDITION P-MMINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
					}
				}
				if (sFound == "MASS-HOLDUP")
				{
					int num = sCompDefList.Count();
					string text6 = "Pot.MmcFresh(";
					string text7 = ") :";
					double num2 = 0.0;
					double num3 = 0.0;
					for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
					{
						text = text6 + "\"" + sCompDefList[k] + "\"" + text7;
						FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
						{
							num3 = 0.0;
							if (double.TryParse(sFound, out num3))
							{
								num2 += num3;
							}
						}
					}
					text3 = GetRowCol("Mass");
					sFound = num2.ToString();
					sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
					item = "\\ INICONDITION P-MMINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
					if (!(num2 < 1E-10))
					{
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
					}
				}
				if (sFound == "MOLE-FRAC")
				{
					text = "POT.MINITIAL :";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						sFound = "10.";
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Initial Conditions: Value of Total initial charge (mole) set to default (10 kmol).");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						text3 = GetRowCol("Moles");
						sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
						item = "\\ INICONDITION P-MINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";

		private void FixSDRStreams(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			if (!bfAddM1_0StreamOut)
			{
				return;
			}
			string sDefEndStage = "";
			string sDefStartStage = "";
			string sNStage = "";
			GetNStageESN(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sDefEndStage, ref sDefStartStage, ref sNStage);
			int count = sCompDefList.Count;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			text2 = "SDRecCompBasis :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = "\\ INICONDITION SDCOMPBASIS = " + sFound + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				text5 = sFound.Trim('"');
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text5))
			{
				text5 = "MOLE-FRAC";
			}
			string text6 = "";
			string text7 = "";
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in sDicSRDLongToShort)
			{
				text6 = item.Key;
				text7 = item.Value;
				text = "\\ PRODUCT SDRECV-NAME = " + text7 + " ";
				text2 = "SDRecv(\"" + text6 + "\").Stage :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = sNStage;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = text + "SDRECV-STAGE = " + sFound + " ";
				}
				text2 = "SDRecv(\"" + text6 + "\").Phase :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					if (text6 != "POTLIQUIDPRODUCT" && text6 != "POTVAPORPRODUCT")
					{
						sFound = "LIQUID";
					}
					if (text6 == "POTLIQUIDPRODUCT")
					{
						sFound = "LIQUID";
					}
					if (text6 == "POTVAPORPRODUCT")
					{
						sFound = "VAPOR";
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = text + "SDRECV-PHASE = " + sFound + " ";
				}
				text2 = "SDRecv(\"" + text6 + "\").FlowBasis :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "MOLE";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = text + "SDRECV-BASIS = " + sFound + " ";
				}
				text = text + "SDRECV-STRM = " + sDicSRDLongNToStreamN[text6] + " \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				text2 = "SDReceivers(\"" + text6 + "\").UpdateIniValuesFromResults :";
				sFound = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					sFound = "FALSE";
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
				{
					text = "\\ SD-COMPOSIT1 1 / SD-RECV1 = " + text7 + " SD-UPDATEINI = " + sFound + " \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
				}
				if (text5 == "MASS-FRAC")
				{
					text2 = "SDReceivers(\"" + text6 + "\").MmInitial :";
					sFound = "";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						sFound = "0.";
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						text3 = GetRowCol("Mass");
						sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
						text = "\\ SD-COMPOSIT1 1 / SD-RECV1 = " + text7 + " SD-MMINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
					}
				}
				if (text5 == "MOLE-FRAC")
				{
					text2 = "SDReceivers(\"" + text6 + "\").MInitial :";
					sFound = "";
					FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						sFound = "0.";
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
					{
						text3 = GetRowCol("Moles");
						sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
						text = "\\ SD-COMPOSIT1 1 / SD-RECV1 = " + text7 + " SD-MINITIAL = " + sFound + text3 + "\\ ";
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
					}
				}
				if (text5 == "MOLE-FRAC")
				{
					text3 = " <0> <0> ";
					text4 = "\\ SD-COMPOSIT2 ";
					text = "";
					for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
					{
						text4 = text4 + "1 SD-CID = " + sCompDefList[i] + " / ";
						text = text + "SD-RECV2 = " + text7 + " SD-CID = " + sCompDefList[i] + " ";
						text2 = "SDReceivers(\"" + text6 + "\").ZInitial(\"" + sCompDefList[i] + "\") :";
						sFound = "";
						FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
						{
							sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
							text = text + "SD-ZINITIAL = " + sFound + text3;
						}
						text = ((i != count - 1) ? (text + "/ ") : (text + "\\ "));
					}
					text = text4 + text;
					string[] array = text.Split('/');
					int num = array.Length;
					if (num > 1)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
						{
							if (j == 0)
							{
								sGlobalBlockList.Add(array[0]);
							}
							else
							{
								sGlobalBlockList.Add("/" + array[j]);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						sGlobalBlockList.Add(text);
					}
				}
				if (text5 == "MOLE-HOLDUP")
				{
					text3 = GetRowCol("Moles");
					text4 = "\\ SD-COMPOSIT2 ";
					text = "";
					for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
					{
						text4 = text4 + "1 SD-CID = " + sCompDefList[k] + " / ";
						text = text + "SD-RECV2 = " + text7 + " SD-CID = " + sCompDefList[k] + " ";
						text2 = "SDReceivers(\"" + text6 + "\").McInitial(\"" + sCompDefList[k] + "\") :";
						sFound = "";
						FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text2, ref sFound);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
						{
							sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);

		private void GetInternalsVar(ref List<string> sIntSecUp, ref string sFindT, ref string sUOMType, ref string sDefValue, ref string sFoundT, ref string sUOMRowCol)
		{
			sFoundT = "";
			sFindT = sFindT.ToUpper();
			sUOMRowCol = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sUOMType))
			{
				sUOMRowCol = GetRowCol(sUOMType);
			}
			int count = sIntSecUp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sIntSecUp[i].Contains(sFindT))
				{
					sFoundT = sIntSecUp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundT))
			{
				sFoundT = sFoundT.Replace(sFindT, "");
				sFoundT = sFoundT.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFoundT))
				{
					sFoundT = sDefValue;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				sFoundT = sDefValue;
			}
			sFoundT = sFoundT.Trim();
		}

		private void FixHoldUp(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "HoldupSec :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string text2 = "";
			int num = 0;
			string text3 = "";
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					text2 = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				num = 0;
				string newValue = "";

					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				text6 = "HoldupBasis :";

		private string GetACMUnitCol(string sUOMType)
		{
			string text = "";
			foreach (UOMtruct mstrCurrUOM in mstrCurrUOMList)
			{
				if (sUOMType.IndexOf(mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 && string.Compare(sUOMType, mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					int mmcol = mstrCurrUOM.mmcol;
					text = mmcol.ToString();
					break;
				}
			}
			return "<" + text + ">";
		}

		private void Fix3Fhase(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "KeyComps :";
			text2 = text2.ToUpper();
			string sFound = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string sFind = "ValidPhases :";
				string sFound2 = "";
				FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref sFind, ref sFound2);
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text2))
				{
					sFound = ((!(sFound2 == "VAPOR-LIQUID")) ? sVariableListUpp[i] : "");
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				text = GetValueR(ref sFound);
				text = text.Trim();
				text = text.TrimStart('[');
				text = text.TrimEnd(']');
				text = text.Replace("\"", "");
				text = text.Replace(",", "");
				text = text.Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = "( " + text + " )";
					string item = "\\ \"L2-COMPS\" KEYCOMPS = " + text + " \\ ";
					sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void FixOperatingSteps(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string sFound = "";
			string sFound2 = "";
			string text = "";
			text = "EndCondition :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "FIXED NUMBER OF BATCHES";
			}
			string text2 = "";
			string item = "\\ \"MULTI-BATCH\" ENDCONDITION = " + sFound switch
			{
				"FIXED NUMBER OF BATCHES" => "FIXED", 
				"MASS HOLDUP CHANGES" => "\"MASS-CHANGE\"", 
				"MOLAR HOLDUP CHANGES" => "\"MOLE-CHANGE\"", 
				_ => "FIXED", 
			} + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			text = "NumberOfBatches :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound2);
			double result = 0.0;
			if (double.TryParse(sFound2, out result) && result > 1.0)
			{
				string item2 = "\\ \"MULTI-BATCH\" BATCHESNO = " + sFound2 + " <0> <0> \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(item2);
			}
			string text3 = "";
			if (sFound == "FIXED NUMBER OF BATCHES" && result <= 1.0)
			{
				text3 = "\\ INICONDITION CHARGETYPE = INITIAL \\ ";
				sGlobalBlockList.Add(text3);
			}
			text = "RelTol :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.01";
			}
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			item = "\\ \"MULTI-BATCH\" RELTOL = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			text = "AbsTol :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.01";
			}
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			item = "\\ \"MULTI-BATCH\" ABSTOL = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			text = "MaxNumberOfBatches :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			sFound = sFound.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "50.";
			}
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			item = "\\ \"MULTI-BATCH\" MAXBATCHES = " + sFound + " <0> <0> \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(item);
			string text4 = "OpSteps :";
			text4 = text4.ToUpper();
			string text5 = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(text4) && sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf(text4) == 0)
				{
					text5 = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Operating Steps:  No Operating Steps. Number of Operating Steps is 0.");
				return;
			}
			string[] array = text5.Split(':');
			if (array.Length > 1)
			{
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				array[1] = array[1].TrimStart('[');
				array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(']');
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				string[] array2 = array[1].Split(',');
				int num = array2.Length;
				if (num > 0)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
					{
						array2[j] = array2[j].Trim();
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(array2[j]))
						{
							list.Add(array2[j]);
						}
					}
					if (list.Count == 0)
					{
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Operating Steps:  No Operating Steps defined.");
						return;
					}
					int count2 = list.Count;
					if (count2 > 1)
					{
						Dictionary<int, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, string>();
						string text6 = "";
						string sFound3 = "";
						for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
						{
							text6 = "OpStep(" + list[k] + ").Index :";
							FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text6, ref sFound3);
							if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound3))
							{
								sFound3 = "1";
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sFound3))
							{
								int result2 = 0;
								if (int.TryParse(sFound3, out result2))
								{
									dictionary.Add(result2, list[k]);
								}
							}
						}
						int count3 = dictionary.Count;
						list.Clear();
						for (int l = 1; l <= count3; l++)
						{
							list.Add(dictionary[l]);
						}
					}
					Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, string>();
					string text7 = "";
					string text8 = "";
					string text9 = "";
					int num2 = 0;
					int num3 = 0;
					for (int m = 0; m < count2; m++)
					{
						text7 = list[m];
						if (text7.Length >= 10)
						{
							text9 = text7.Substring(0, 5);
							text9 += num3;
							num3++;
							text8 = text9 + "\"";
							if (dictionary2.ContainsValue(text8))
							{
								text9 += num2;
								num2++;
							}
							text9 += "\"";
							dictionary2.Add(text7, text9);
						}
						else if (dictionary2.ContainsValue(text7))
						{
							text9 = text7.Substring(0, 5);
							text9 += num3;
							num3++;
							text8 = text9 + "\"";
							if (dictionary2.ContainsValue(text8))
							{
								text9 += num2;
								num2++;
							}
							text9 += "\"";
							dictionary2.Add(text7, text9);
						}
						else
						{
							dictionary2.Add(text7, text7);
						}
					}
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item3 in dictionary2)
					{
						if (item3.Key != item3.Value)
						{
							lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Operating Step " + item3.Key + " renamed to " + item3.Value + ".");
						}
					}
					int count4 = list.Count;
					if (count4 == 0)
					{
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Operating Steps:  No Operating Steps defined.");
						return;
					}
					sOpStepListUpp.Clear();
					int count5 = sVariableListUpp.Count;
					string text10 = "OpStep(";
					text10 = text10.ToUpper();
					text4 = "";
					for (int n = 0; n < count4; n++)
					{
						text4 = text10 + list[n] + ").";
						for (int num4 = 0; num4 < count5; num4++)
						{
							if (sVariableListUpp[num4].IndexOf(text4, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
							{
								sOpStepListUpp.Add(sVariableListUpp[num4]);
							}
						}
					}
					int count6 = sOpStepListUpp.Count;
					if (count6 == 0)
					{
						string text11 = "";
						string text12 = "";
						for (int num5 = 0; num5 < count4; num5++)
						{
							text12 = list[num5];
							text11 = "\\ OP-STEP OPS-NAME = " + dictionary2[text12] + " OP-ACTIVE = NO \\ ";
							sGlobalBlockList.Add(text11);
						}
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Operating Steps:  No active Operating Steps found.");
						return;
					}
					string text13 = "";

		private void GetTriggerSDReceiver(ref string sStep, ref string sTrigSDRsvr)
		{
			sTrigSDRsvr = "";
			string text = "OpStep(" + sStep + ").TriggerSDReceiver :";
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string sFound = "";
			int count = sOpStepListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sOpStepListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					sFound = sOpStepListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sTrigSDRsvr = GetValueR(ref sFound);
			}
		}

		private void ParseTiggerComponentW(ref string sLeftC, ref string sStep, ref string sRightC, ref string sComp)
		{
			string sUOM = "";
			string sDefVal = "";
			string sUOMRC = "";
			ParseTiggerVar(ref sUOM, ref sLeftC, ref sStep, ref sRightC, ref sDefVal, ref sComp, ref sUOMRC);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sComp))
			{
				sComp = "TRIG-COMP = " + sComp;
			}
		}

		private void ParseTiggerVar(ref string sUOM, ref string sLeft, ref string sStep, ref string sRight, ref string sDefVal, ref string sValR, ref string sUOMRC)
		{
			sValR = "";
			sUOMRC = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sUOM))
			{
				sUOMRC = GetRowCol(sUOM);
				sUOMRC = sUOMRC.Trim();
			}
			string text = sLeft + sStep + sRight;
			text = text.ToUpper();
			string sFound = "";
			int count = sOpStepListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sOpStepListUpp[i].Contains(text))
				{
					sFound = sOpStepListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sValR = sDefVal;
				return;
			}
			sValR = GetValueR(ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sValR))
			{
				sValR = sDefVal;
			}
		}

		private bool GetIndexR(ref string sFound, ref string sIndx)
		{
			bool result = true;
			sIndx = "";
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int result2 = 0;
			string[] array = sFound.Split(':');
			if (array.Length > 1)
			{
				num = array[0].LastIndexOf('(');
				num2 = array[0].LastIndexOf(')');
				if (num <= 0 || num2 <= 0 || num2 <= num)
				{
					return false;
				}
				sIndx = array[0].Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
				sIndx.Trim();
				if (!int.TryParse(sIndx, out result2))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetValueR(ref string sFound)
		{
			string result = "";
			string[] array = sFound.Split(':');
			int num = array.Length;
			if (num > 1)
			{
				array[num - 1] = array[num - 1].Trim();
				array[num - 1] = array[num - 1].TrimEnd(';');
				result = array[num - 1].Trim();
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void FixControllers(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			dictionary.Add("COILS HEATING DUTY", "PVTYPE = \"COIL-HDUTY\"");
			dictionary.Add("COILS HEATING T DIFF", "PVTYPE = \"COIL-HDELT\"");
			dictionary.Add("COLUMN PRESSURE DROP", "PVTYPE = \"COL-PDROP\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER LIQUID MASS FLOW RATE", "PVTYPE = \"C-L-MASSFLOW\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER LIQUID MOLAR FLOW RATE", "PVTYPE = \"C-L-MOLEFLOW\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER MASS FRACTION", "PVTYPE = \"C-MASSFRAC\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER MOLE FRACTION", "PVTYPE = \"C-MOLEFRAC\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER TEMPERATURE", "PVTYPE = \"COND-TEMP\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER VAPOR MASS FLOW RATE", "PVTYPE = \"C-V-MASSFLOW\"");
			dictionary.Add("CONDENSER VAPOR MOLAR FLOW RATE", "PVTYPE = \"C-V-MOLEFLOW\"");
			dictionary.Add("DISTILLATE MASS FRACTION", "PVTYPE = \"D-MASSFRAC\"");
			dictionary.Add("DISTILLATE MOLE FRACTION", "PVTYPE = \"D-MOLEFRAC\"");
			dictionary.Add("POT LIQUID LEVEL", "PVTYPE = \"P-LIQLEVEL\"");
			dictionary.Add("POT LIQUID MASS HOLDUP", "PVTYPE = \"P-MASSHOLDUP\"");
			dictionary.Add("POT LIQUID MOLE HOLDUP", "PVTYPE = \"P-MOLEHOLDUP\"");
			dictionary.Add("POT LIQUID VOLUME", "PVTYPE = \"P-LIQVOLUME\"");

		private void FixPotGeometry(ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref List<string> sGlobalBlockList)
		{
			string text = "";
			string sFound = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			text = "Pot.Orientation :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "VERTICAL";
			}
			text2 = "\\ POT ORIENTATION = " + sFound + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.GeometrySpec :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "DL";
			}
			if (sFound == "LD")
			{
				sFound = "DL";
			}
			text2 = "\\ POT GEOMETRYSPEC = " + sFound + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.TopHead :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "ELLIPTICAL";
			}
			sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
			text2 = "\\ POT TOPHEAD = " + sFound + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.BottomHead :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "ELLIPTICAL";
			}
			sFound = sFound.Trim('"');
			text2 = "\\ POT BOTTOMHEAD = " + sFound + " \\ ";
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.D :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "1.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of Diameter is set to default (1.0 m).");
			}
			text3 = GetRowCol("Length");
			text3 = text3.Trim();
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT D = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.L :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "1.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of Height is set to default (1.0 m).");
			}
			text3 = GetRowCol("Length");
			text3 = text3.Trim();
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT L = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.fD :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "1.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of Dish radius ratio set to default (1.0).");
			}
			text3 = "<0> <0>";
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT FD = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.kD :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.06";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of Knuckle radius ratio is set to default (0.06).");
			}
			text3 = "<0> <0>";
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT KD = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.V :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "1.";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of volume is set to default (1 cum).");
			}
			text3 = GetRowCol("Length");
			text3 = text3.Trim();
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT V = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
			text = "Pot.ConeHeight :";
			FindLineVal(ref sVariableListUpp, ref text, ref sFound);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sFound))
			{
				sFound = "0.5";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Pot Geometry: Value of Cone Height is set to default (0.5 m).");
			}
			text3 = GetRowCol("Length");
			text3 = text3.Trim();
			sFound = TrancateTo18(sFound);
			text2 = "\\ POT CONEHEIGHT = # ? \\ ";
			text2 = text2.Replace("#", sFound);
			text2 = text2.Replace("?", text3);
			sGlobalBlockList.Add(text2);
		}

		private void FindVariableLine(ref string sFind, ref List<string> sVariableListUpp, ref string sFound)
		{
			sFind = sFind.ToUpper();
			sFound = "";
			int count = sVariableListUpp.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sVariableListUpp[i].Contains(sFind) && sVariableListUpp[i].IndexOf(sFind, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					sFound = sVariableListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ParseAndCreateVariableBkpStringList(ref List<string> sVarListUpp, ref List<string> sVarListBkp)
		{
			List<VariableStruct> list = new List<VariableStruct>();
			List<VariableStruct> list2 = new List<VariableStruct>();
			List<VariableStruct> list3 = new List<VariableStruct>();
			List<VariableStruct> list4 = new List<VariableStruct>();
			int num = VariableTable.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				string acmvar = VariableTable[i].acmvar;
				if (acmvar.Contains("(*)") && acmvar.Contains("(#)"))
				{
					list4.Add(VariableTable[i]);
				}
				else if (acmvar.Contains("(*).") && !acmvar.Contains("(#)"))
				{
					list3.Add(VariableTable[i]);
				}
				else if (acmvar.Contains("(*)") && !acmvar.Contains("(#)"))
				{
					list2.Add(VariableTable[i]);
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(VariableTable[i]);
				}
			}

		private void GetAstAndNum(ref string sIn3, ref string sAst3, ref string sNum3, ref string sValOut)
		{
			sAst3 = "";
			sNum3 = "";
			int num = sIn3.IndexOf('(');
			int num2 = sIn3.IndexOf(')');
			sAst3 = sIn3.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
			string text = sIn3.Substring(num2 + 2);
			num = text.IndexOf('(');
			num2 = text.IndexOf(')');
			sNum3 = text.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
			sValOut = "";
			string[] array = sIn3.Split(':');
			if (array.Length == 2)
			{
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				sValOut = array[1];
			}
		}

		private bool GetIndxStage(ref string sIn, ref string sIndx, ref string sStage, ref bool bfStart)
		{
			bool result = true;
			sIndx = "";
			sStage = "";
			int length = "HOLDUPSECTIONS(".Length;
			string value = ").STARTSTAGE";
			string value2 = ").ENDSTAGE";
			string[] array = sIn.Split(':');
			int num = array.Length;
			string text = "";
			if (num == 2)
			{
				sStage = array[1];
				sStage = sStage.Trim();
				sStage = sStage.TrimEnd(';');
				sStage = sStage.Trim();
				if (!int.TryParse(sStage, out var result2))
				{
					return false;
				}
				array[0] = array[0].Trim();
				text = array[0].Substring(length);
				if (text.IndexOf(value, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) > 0)
				{
					bfStart = true;
					result2 = text.IndexOf(value, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
					sIndx = text.Substring(0, result2);
					sIndx = sIndx.Trim();
					if (!int.TryParse(sIndx, out result2))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (text.IndexOf(value2, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) <= 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
					bfStart = false;
					result2 = text.IndexOf(value2, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
					sIndx = text.Substring(0, result2);
					sIndx = sIndx.Trim();
					if (!int.TryParse(sIndx, out result2))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void GetSentValue(int itype, ref string suomtype, ref string sVarName, ref string sVal, ref string sSentVal, ref string sTempUOMType)
		{
			sSentVal = "";
			if (suomtype == "S")
			{
				switch (itype)
				{
				case 1:
				case 3:
				case 4:
				case 5:
					sSentVal = sVarName + " = " + sVal;
					break;
				case 6:
					sSentVal = sVarName + " = \"" + sVal + "\"";
					break;
				case 7:
					if (sVal.Length > 8)
					{
						sSentVal = sVarName + " = \"" + sVal + "\"";
					}
					else
					{
						sSentVal = sVarName + " = " + sVal;
					}
					break;
				}
				return;
			}
			if (suomtype == "00")
			{
				sVal = TrancateTo18(sVal);
				sSentVal = sVarName + " = " + sVal + " <0> <0>";
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sTempUOMType))
			{
				foreach (UOMtruct mstrCurrUOM in mstrCurrUOMList)
				{
					if (suomtype.IndexOf(mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
					{
						string[] obj = new string[5] { " <", null, null, null, null };
						int mmrow = mstrCurrUOM.mmrow;
						obj[1] = mmrow.ToString();
						obj[2] = "> <";
						mmrow = mstrCurrUOM.mmcol;
						obj[3] = mmrow.ToString();
						obj[4] = ">";
						text = string.Concat(obj);
						break;
					}
				}
				sVal = TrancateTo18(sVal);
				sSentVal = sVarName + " = " + sVal + text;
				return;
			}
			string text2 = "";
			UOMtruct[] uOMTable = UOMTable;
			for (int mmrow = 0; mmrow < uOMTable.Length; mmrow++)
			{
				UOMtruct uOMtruct = uOMTable[mmrow];
				text2 = uOMtruct.acmUOMsymbol;
				text2 = text2.ToUpper();
				if (sTempUOMType == text2)
				{
					string[] obj2 = new string[5] { " <", null, null, null, null };
					int mmrow2 = uOMtruct.mmrow;
					obj2[1] = mmrow2.ToString();
					obj2[2] = "> <";
					mmrow2 = uOMtruct.mmcol;
					obj2[3] = mmrow2.ToString();
					obj2[4] = ">";
					text = string.Concat(obj2);
					break;
				}
			}
			sVal = TrancateTo18(sVal);
			sSentVal = sVarName + " = " + sVal + text;
		}

		private bool ParseVarUppD(ref string sIn, ref string sRight, ref string sAstOut, ref string sValOut)
		{
			bool result = true;
			sAstOut = "";
			sValOut = "";
			string[] array = sIn.Split(':');
			if (array.Length != 2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = sIn.IndexOf('(');
			int num2 = sIn.IndexOf(')');
			if (num <= 0 || num2 <= 0 || num >= num2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			array[0] = array[0].Trim();
			array[1] = array[1].Trim();
			array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
			array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
			sValOut = array[1];
			array[0].Substring(0, num);
			if (string.Compare(array[0].Substring(num2), sRight, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			sAstOut = array[0].Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
			return result;
		}

		private void GetIndex(ref string sIn, ref string sOutI)
		{
			sOutI = "";
			sIn = sIn.Trim();
			int length = sIn.Length;
			int num = sIn.IndexOf("(", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			int num2 = sIn.IndexOf(")", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
			if (num > 0 && num2 == length - 1)
			{
				sIn = sIn.TrimEnd(')');
				sOutI = sIn.Substring(num + 1);
			}
		}

		private void ParseVar1Value(ref string sIn, ref string sOutN, ref string sOutV)
		{
			sOutN = "";
			sOutV = "";
			string[] array = sIn.Split(':');
			int num = array.Length;
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				sOutN = array[0].Trim();
			}
			if (num >= 1)
			{
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
				array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
				sOutV = array[1];
			}
		}

		private void GetVar1Value(ref string sIn, ref string sOut)
		{
			sOut = "";
			string[] array = sIn.Split(':');
			if (array.Length >= 1)
			{
				array[1] = array[1].Trim();
				array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
				array[1] = array[1].Trim('"');
				sOut = array[1];
			}
		}

		private bool GetBkpStrngVarStructType1(ref string sVarVal1, ref VariableStruct TempVarStructl, ref string sRetStrg)
		{
			bool result = true;
			sRetStrg = TempVarStructl.mmvar + " = ";
			int type = TempVarStructl.type;
			string sIn = TempVarStructl.uomtype;
			string text = "";
			if (type == 2 || type == 22)
			{
				if (sIn == "00")
				{
					sVarVal1 = TrancateTo18(sVarVal1);
					text = sVarVal1 + " <0> <0> ";
				}
				else if (sIn.IndexOf("UNIT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
				{
					string sOut = "";
					GetVar1Value(ref sIn, ref sOut);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sOut))
					{
						return false;
					}
					text = "";
					string text2 = "";
					UOMtruct[] uOMTable = UOMTable;
					for (int i = 0; i < uOMTable.Length; i++)
					{
						UOMtruct uOMtruct = uOMTable[i];
						text2 = uOMtruct.acmUOMsymbol;
						text2 = text2.ToUpper();
						if (sOut == text2)
						{
							sVarVal1 = TrancateTo18(sVarVal1);
							string[] obj = new string[6] { sVarVal1, " <", null, null, null, null };
							int mmrow = uOMtruct.mmrow;
							obj[2] = mmrow.ToString();
							obj[3] = "> <";
							mmrow = uOMtruct.mmcol;
							obj[4] = mmrow.ToString();
							obj[5] = "> ";
							text = string.Concat(obj);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					text = "";
					foreach (UOMtruct mstrCurrUOM in mstrCurrUOMList)
					{
						if (sIn.IndexOf(mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
						{
							sVarVal1 = TrancateTo18(sVarVal1);
							string[] obj2 = new string[6] { sVarVal1, " <", null, null, null, null };
							int i = mstrCurrUOM.mmrow;
							obj2[2] = i.ToString();
							obj2[3] = "> <";
							i = mstrCurrUOM.mmcol;
							obj2[4] = i.ToString();
							obj2[5] = "> ";
							text = string.Concat(obj2);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if ((type == 1 || type == 3 || type == 4 || type == 5) && sIn == "S")
			{
				if (sVarVal1 == "FALSE")
				{
					sVarVal1 = "0";
				}
				if (sVarVal1 == "TRUE")
				{
					sVarVal1 = "1";
				}
				text = sVarVal1 + " ";
			}
			if (type == 6 && sIn == "S")
			{
				text = "\"" + sVarVal1 + "\" ";
			}
			if (type == 7 && sIn == "S")
			{
				text = ((sVarVal1.Length <= 8) ? (sVarVal1 + " ") : ("\"" + sVarVal1 + "\" "));
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return false;
			}
			sRetStrg += text;
			return result;
		}

		private void bClearMsgs_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			lstMsgBox.Items.Clear();
		}

		private string VariableString(ref string sName, ref string sValue)
		{
			return "/ NAME = \"" + sName + "\" VALUE = \"" + sValue + "\" / ";
		}

		private string GetACMUOMu(ref string[] sArrRowCol, string sBaseUOM)
		{
			string text = sArrRowCol[0].TrimStart('<');
			text = text.TrimEnd('>');
			string text2 = sArrRowCol[1].TrimStart('<');
			text2 = text2.TrimEnd('>');
			int num = UOMTable.Length;
			string result = "";
			string text3 = sBaseUOM.ToUpper();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				if (UOMTable[i].acmUOMtype.ToUpper() == text3 && text == UOMTable[i].mmrow.ToString() && text2 == UOMTable[i].mmcol.ToString())
				{
					result = UOMTable[i].acmUOMsymbol;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void GetMatStreamSents(string sStrmName, ref List<string> sStreamBkpLines, ref List<string> sChargeListUpp)
		{
			sStreamBkpLines.Clear();
			string text = sDicStreamLongToShort[sStrmName];
			if (sStrmName == sDummyStream)
			{
				sStrmName = text;
				text = sDummyStream;
			}
			string item = sMatStreamHeader.Replace("*", text);
			sStreamBkpLines.Add(item);
			string item2 = "\\ SUBSTREAM SSID = MIXED  MIXED-SPEC = TP ";
			sStreamBkpLines.Add(item2);
			string text2 = "";
			int count = sChargeListUpp.Count;
			string value = "CHARGE(\"" + sStrmName + "\").T :";
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (sChargeListUpp[i].Contains(value))
				{
					text2 = sChargeListUpp[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			string rowCol = GetRowCol("Temperature");
			bool flag = false;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				string[] array = text2.Split(':');
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					flag = true;
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					string item3 = "TEMP = " + array[1] + rowCol;
					sStreamBkpLines.Add(item3);
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				string text3 = "25.0";
				string item4 = "TEMP = " + text3 + " <22> <4> ";
				sStreamBkpLines.Add(item4);
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Temperature set to default (25 C).");
			}
			string text4 = "";
			value = "CHARGE(\"" + sStrmName + "\").P :";
			for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
			{
				if (sChargeListUpp[j].Contains(value))
				{
					text4 = sChargeListUpp[j];
					break;
				}
			}
			rowCol = GetRowCol("Pressure");
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				string[] array2 = text4.Split(':');
				if (array2.Length == 2)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					array2[1] = array2[1].Trim();
					array2[1] = array2[1].TrimEnd(';');
					array2[1] = array2[1].Trim();
					string item5 = "PRES = " + array2[1] + rowCol;
					sStreamBkpLines.Add(item5);
				}
			}
			if (!flag2)
			{
				string text5 = "1.01325";
				string item6 = "PRES = " + text5 + " <20> <5> ";
				sStreamBkpLines.Add(item6);
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Pressure set to default (1.01325 bar).");
			}
			string sIn = "";
			value = "CHARGE(\"" + sStrmName + "\").FLOWBASIS :";
			for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
			{
				if (sChargeListUpp[k].Contains(value))
				{
					sIn = sChargeListUpp[k];
					break;
				}
			}
			string value2 = GetValue(ref sIn);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2))
			{
				value2 = value2.Trim('"');
			}
			else
			{
				value2 = "MOLE";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Total Flow Basis set to default (Mole).");
			}
			string text6 = "FLOWBASE = " + value2 + " ";
			string text7 = "";
			string text8 = "";
			if (value2 == "MASS")
			{
				text7 = "<-80>";
				text8 = "MassFlow";
			}
			if (value2 == "MOLE")
			{
				text7 = "<-89>";
				text8 = "MoleFlow";
			}
			if (value2 == "VOLUME")
			{
				text7 = "<-99>";
				text8 = "VolumetricFlow";
			}
			rowCol = GetRowCol(text8);
			rowCol = rowCol.Trim();
			string[] sArrRowCol = rowCol.Split(' ');
			string aCMUOMu = GetACMUOMu(ref sArrRowCol, text8);
			string item7 = text6 + "TOTFLOW = 1. " + text7 + " " + sArrRowCol[1] + " ";
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(aCMUOMu))
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value (1.0) of Total flow rate is artificial.");
			}
			else
			{
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value (1.0 " + aCMUOMu + ") of Total flow rate is artificial.");
			}
			sStreamBkpLines.Add(item7);
			value = "Charge(\"" + sStrmName + "\").CompBasis :";
			value = value.ToUpper();
			string sIn2 = "";
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				if (sChargeListUpp[l].Contains(value))
				{
					sIn2 = sChargeListUpp[l];
					break;
				}
			}
			bool flag3 = false;
			string text9 = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sIn2))
			{
				text9 = GetValue(ref sIn2);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text9))
				{
					text9 = "\"MOLE-FRAC\"";
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value (MOLE-FRAC) of Composition basis set to default (MOLE-FRAC).");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text9 = "\"MOLE-FRAC\"";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Composition basis set to default (MOLE-FRAC).");
			}
			text9 = text9.Trim('"');
			string text10 = "";
			if (!bfPotOverCond)
			{
				text10 = "";
				value = "Charge(\"" + sStrmName + "\").ValidPhases";
				value = value.ToUpper();
				for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
				{
					if (sChargeListUpp[m].Contains(value))
					{
						text10 = sChargeListUpp[m];
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text10 = "";
				value = "ChargeValidPhases(\"" + sStrmName + "\")";
				value = value.ToUpper();
				for (int n = 0; n < count; n++)
				{
					if (sChargeListUpp[n].Contains(value))
					{
						text10 = sChargeListUpp[n];
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			string text11 = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text10))
			{

				text11 = text11.Trim('"');
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text11))
				{
					text11 = "LIQUID-ONLY";
					lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Valid Phases set to default (LIQUID-ONLY).");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text11 = "LIQUID-ONLY";
				lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Valid Phases set to default (LIQUID-ONLY).");
			}

			}

			{
				item8 = "FREE-WATER = NO NPHASE = 2 PHASE = V ";
			}
			if (text11 == "VAPOR-ONLY")
			{
				item8 = "FREE-WATER = NO NPHASE = 1 PHASE = V ";
			}
			sStreamBkpLines.Add(item8);
			if (!flag3)
			{
				if (text9 == "MOLE-FRAC")
				{
					string text12 = "Charge(\"" + sStrmName + "\").ZR(";
					text12 = text12.ToUpper();
					string value3 = ") :";
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					for (int num = 0; num < count; num++)
					{
						if (sChargeListUpp[num].Contains(text12) && sChargeListUpp[num].Contains(value3))
						{
							list.Add(sChargeListUpp[num]);
						}
					}
					int count2 = list.Count;
					if (count2 > 0)
					{
						List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
						string text13 = "";
						string text14 = "";
						double num2 = 0.0;
						string text15 = "SSID1 = MIXED CID = * FLOW = # <-4> <0>";
						string text16 = "";
						for (int num3 = 0; num3 < count2; num3++)
						{
							text13 = list[num3];
							text13 = text13.Replace(text12, "");
							text13 = text13.Replace(")", "");
							text14 = GetValue(ref text13);
							text14 = text14.Trim();
							text13 = text13.Split(':')[0].Trim();
							text13 = text13.Trim('"');
							text13 = text13.Trim();
							num2 += Convert.ToDouble(text14);
							text16 = text15.Replace("*", text13);
							text14 = TrancateTo18(text14);
							text16 = text16.Replace("#", text14);
							list2.Add(text16);
						}
						string sIn3 = num2.ToString();
						sIn3 = TrancateTo18(sIn3);
						string item9 = "BASIS = \"MOLE-FRAC\" TOTAL = " + sIn3 + " <0> <0> JUNK = " + count2 + " \\ ";
						sStreamBkpLines.Add(item9);
						for (int num4 = 0; num4 < count2; num4++)
						{
							item9 = ((num4 == 0) ? "\\ MOLE-FLOW " : "/ ");
							item9 = item9 + list2[num4] + " ";
							sStreamBkpLines.Add(item9);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						sStreamBkpLines.Add("BASIS = \"MOLE-FRAC\"");
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + " (" + text9 + "): Composition component list is missing. Invalid file.");
					}
				}
				if (text9 == "MASS-FRAC")
				{
					string text17 = "Charge(\"" + sStrmName + "\").ZmR(";
					text17 = text17.ToUpper();
					string value4 = ") :";
					List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
					for (int num5 = 0; num5 < count; num5++)
					{
						if (sChargeListUpp[num5].Contains(text17) && sChargeListUpp[num5].Contains(value4))
						{
							list3.Add(sChargeListUpp[num5]);
						}
					}
					int count3 = list3.Count;
					if (count3 > 0)
					{
						List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
						string text18 = "";
						string text19 = "";
						double num6 = 0.0;
						string text20 = "SSID1 = MIXED CID = * FLOW = # <-3> <0>";
						string text21 = "";
						for (int num7 = 0; num7 < count3; num7++)
						{
							text18 = list3[num7];
							text18 = text18.Replace(text17, "");
							text18 = text18.Replace(")", "");
							text19 = GetValue(ref text18);
							text19 = text19.Trim();
							text18 = text18.Split(':')[0].Trim();
							text18 = text18.Trim('"');
							text18 = text18.Trim();
							num6 += Convert.ToDouble(text19);
							text21 = text20.Replace("*", text18);
							text19 = TrancateTo18(text19);
							text21 = text21.Replace("#", text19);
							list4.Add(text21);
						}
						string sIn4 = num6.ToString();
						sIn4 = TrancateTo18(sIn4);
						string item10 = "BASIS = \"MASS-FRAC\" TOTAL = " + sIn4 + " <0> <0> JUNK = " + count3 + " \\ ";
						sStreamBkpLines.Add(item10);
						for (int num8 = 0; num8 < count3; num8++)
						{
							item10 = ((num8 == 0) ? "\\ MOLE-FLOW " : "/ ");
							item10 = item10 + list4[num8] + " ";
							sStreamBkpLines.Add(item10);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						sStreamBkpLines.Add("BASIS = \"MASS-FRAC\"");
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + " (" + text9 + "): Composition component list is missing. Invalid file");
					}
				}
				if (text9 == "MOLE-CONC")
				{
					string text22 = "Charge(";
					text22 += "\"";
					text22 += sStrmName;
					text22 += "\"";
					text22 += ").C(";
					text22 = text22.ToUpper();
					string value5 = ") :";
					List<string> list5 = new List<string>();
					for (int num9 = 0; num9 < count; num9++)
					{
						if (sChargeListUpp[num9].Contains(text22) && sChargeListUpp[num9].Contains(value5))
						{
							list5.Add(sChargeListUpp[num9]);
						}
					}
					int count4 = list5.Count;
					if (count4 > 0)
					{
						string[] array3 = GetRowCol("MolarDensity").Trim().Split(' ');
						array3[1] = array3[1].Trim();
						List<string> list6 = new List<string>();
						string text23 = "";
						string text24 = "";
						double num10 = 0.0;
						string text25 = "SSID1 = MIXED CID = * FLOW = # <-519> " + array3[1];
						string text26 = "";
						for (int num11 = 0; num11 < count4; num11++)
						{
							text23 = list5[num11];
							text23 = text23.Replace(text22, "");
							text23 = text23.Replace(")", "");
							text24 = GetValue(ref text23);
							text24 = text24.Trim();
							text23 = text23.Split(':')[0].Trim();
							text23 = text23.Trim('"');
							text23 = text23.Trim();
							num10 += Convert.ToDouble(text24);
							text26 = text25.Replace("*", text23);
							text24 = TrancateTo18(text24);
							text26 = text26.Replace("#", text24);
							list6.Add(text26);
						}
						string sIn5 = num10.ToString();
						sIn5 = TrancateTo18(sIn5);
						string item11 = "BASIS = \"MOLE-CONC\" TOTAL = " + sIn5 + " <-89> " + array3[1] + " JUNK = " + count4 + " \\ ";
						sStreamBkpLines.Add(item11);
						for (int num12 = 0; num12 < count4; num12++)
						{
							item11 = ((num12 == 0) ? "\\ MOLE-FLOW " : "/ ");
							item11 = item11 + list6[num12] + " ";
							sStreamBkpLines.Add(item11);
						}
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + ": Value of Solvent is not defined.");
					}
					else
					{
						sStreamBkpLines.Add("BASIS = \"MOLE-CONC\"");
						lstMsgBox.Items.Add("Stream " + sStrmName + " (" + text9 + "): Composition component list is missing. Invalid file.");
					}
				}
				if (text9 == "MASS-CONC")
				{
					string text27 = "Charge(\"" + sStrmName + "\").Cm(";
					text27 = text27.ToUpper();
					string value6 = ") :";
					List<string> list7 = new List<string>();
					for (int num13 = 0; num13 < count; num13++)
					{
						if (sChargeListUpp[num13].Contains(text27) && sChargeListUpp[num13].Contains(value6))
						{
							list7.Add(sChargeListUpp[num13]);
						}
					}
					int count5 = list7.Count;
					if (count5 > 0)
					{
						string[] array4 = GetRowCol("MassDensity").Trim().Split(' ');
						array4[1] = array4[1].Trim();
						List<string> list8 = new List<string>();
						string text28 = "";
						string text29 = "";
						double num14 = 0.0;
						string text30 = "SSID1 = MIXED CID = * FLOW = # <-470> " + array4[1];
						string text31 = "";
						for (int num15 = 0; num15 < count5; num15++)
						{
							text28 = list7[num15];
							text28 = text28.Replace(text27, "");
							text28 = text28.Replace(")", "");
							text29 = GetValue(ref text28);
							text29 = text29.Trim();
							text28 = text28.Split(':')[0].Trim();
							text28 = text28.Trim('"');
							text28 = text28.Trim();
							num14 += Convert.ToDouble(text29);
							text31 = text30.Replace("*", text28);
							text29 = TrancateTo18(text29);
							text31 = text31.Replace("#", text29);
							list8.Add(text31);
						}

						sIn6 = TrancateTo18(sIn6);
						string item12 = "BASIS = \"MASS-CONC\" TOTAL = " + sIn6 + " <-89> " + array4[1] + " JUNK = " + count5 + " \\ ";

					}
				}
			}
			sStreamBkpLines.Add("\\ ");
		}

		private string GetValue(ref string sIn)
		{
			string result = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sIn))
			{
				string[] array = sIn.Split(':');
				if (array.Length == 2)
				{
					array[1] = array[1].Trim();
					array[1] = array[1].TrimEnd(';');
					result = array[1].Trim();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetRowCol(string sUOMType)
		{
			string result = "";
			foreach (UOMtruct mstrCurrUOM in mstrCurrUOMList)
			{
				if (sUOMType.IndexOf(mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 && string.Compare(sUOMType, mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					string[] obj = new string[5] { " <", null, null, null, null };
					int mmrow = mstrCurrUOM.mmrow;
					obj[1] = mmrow.ToString();
					obj[2] = "> <";
					mmrow = mstrCurrUOM.mmcol;
					obj[3] = mmrow.ToString();
					obj[4] = "> ";
					result = string.Concat(obj);
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetACMUnit(string sUOMType)
		{
			string result = "";
			foreach (UOMtruct mstrCurrUOM in mstrCurrUOMList)
			{
				if (sUOMType.IndexOf(mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 && string.Compare(sUOMType, mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMtype, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					result = mstrCurrUOM.acmUOMsymbol;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string TrancateTo18(string sIn)
		{
			string text = "";
			text = sIn;
			text = text.Trim();
			text = text.Trim('"');
			if (text.Length > 18 && double.TryParse(text, out var _))
			{
				int num = text.LastIndexOf("E", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
				if (num > 0)
				{
					string text2 = text.Substring(0, num - 1);
					string text3 = text.Substring(num);
					_ = text2.Length;
					int length = text3.Length;
					int length2 = 18 - length - 1;
					text = text2.Substring(0, length2) + text3;
				}
				else
				{
					text = text.Substring(0, 17);
				}
			}
			return text;
		}
